{"type":"assistant","message":{"id":"05c782ace8f7ae75f1b1fed8eef58834","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"call_function_r3tsr053g48d_1","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/root/seismic_phase_association.py","content":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"\nSeismic Phase Association Script\nLoads waveform data and station information, picks P and S waves,\nand associates them to identify earthquake events.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport obspy\nimport pandas as pd\nimport numpy as np\nfrom datetime import datetime, timedelta\nimport seisbench\nimport seisbench.models as sbm\nfrom collections import defaultdict\nimport warnings\nwarnings.filterwarnings('ignore')\n\ndef load_data():\n    \"\"\"Load waveform and station data.\"\"\"\n    print(\"Loading waveform data...\")\n    stream = obspy.read('/root/data/wave.mseed')\n    print(f\"Loaded {len(stream)} traces\")\n\n    print(\"Loading station data...\")\n    stations_df = pd.read_csv('/root/data/stations.csv')\n    print(f\"Loaded {len(stations_df)} station channels\")\n\n    return stream, stations_df\n\ndef prepare_streams_for_picking(stream, stations_df):\n    \"\"\"Prepare streams for phase picking by grouping by station.\"\"\"\n    # Create station dictionary\n    station_dict = {}\n    for _, row in stations_df.iterrows():\n        key = f\"{row['network']}.{row['station']}\"\n        if key not in station_dict:\n            station_dict[key] = {\n                'longitude': row['longitude'],\n                'latitude': row['latitude'],\n                'elevation_m': row['elevation_m']\n            }\n\n    # Group traces by station\n    station_streams = defaultdict(list)\n    for trace in stream:\n        # Get station info\n        network = trace.stats.network\n        station = trace.stats.station\n        key = f\"{network}.{station}\"\n\n        # Get channel\n        channel = trace.stats.channel\n\n        # Only keep vertical (Z) channels for picking\n        if channel.endswith('Z'):\n            station_streams[key].append(trace)\n\n    # Convert to list of streams\n    streams_list = []\n    for station_id, traces in station_streams.items():\n        if len(traces) > 0:\n            st = obspy.Stream(traces)\n            st.merge(fill_value=0)\n            if station_id in station_dict:\n                st._metadata = station_dict[station_id]\n            streams_list.append(st)\n\n    print(f\"Prepared {len(streams_list)} station streams for picking\")\n    return streams_list\n\ndef pick_phases(streams_list):\n    \"\"\"Pick P and S phases using SeisBench model.\"\"\"\n    print(\"Loading SeisBench EQTransformer model...\")\n    model = sbm.EQTransformer()\n\n    print(\"Starting phase picking...\")\n    # The model will pick P and S phases on the waveforms\n    picks_dict = model.annotate(streams_list)\n\n    all_picks = []\n    for station_id, picks in picks_dict.items():\n        for pick in picks:\n            all_picks.append({\n                'station_id': station_id,\n                'time': pick.time.datetime,\n                'phase_type': pick.phase.lower(),\n                'probability': pick.get('probability', 1.0)\n            })\n\n    print(f\"Found {len(all_picks)} picks\")\n    return all_picks\n\ndef associate_phases(picks, stations_df, vp=6.0, vs=None):\n    \"\"\"\n    Associate P and S picks to identify earthquake events.\n    Uses a simple time-window based association algorithm.\n    \"\"\"\n    if vs is None:\n        vs = vp / 1.75\n\n    print(\"Starting phase association...\")\n\n    # Create station lookup\n    station_lookup = {}\n    for _, row in stations_df.iterrows():\n        key = f\"{row['network']}.{row['station']}\"\n        station_lookup[key] = {\n            'longitude': row['longitude'],\n            'latitude': row['latitude']\n        }\n\n    # Sort picks by time\n    picks_sorted = sorted(picks, key=lambda x: x['time'])\n\n    # Group picks into potential events using time windows\n    events = []\n    current_event_picks = []\n    event_start_time = None\n\n    # Time window for grouping picks (seconds)\n    time_window = 30.0\n\n    for pick in picks_sorted:\n        if event_start_time is None:\n            event_start_time = pick['time']\n            current_event_picks = [pick]\n        elif (pick['time'] - event_start_time).total_seconds() < time_window:\n            current_event_picks.append(pick)\n        else:\n            # Process current event\n            if len(current_event_picks) >= 3:  # Need at least 3 picks\n                event = associate_event_picks(current_event_picks, station_lookup, vp, vs)\n                if event:\n                    events.append(event)\n\n            # Start new event\n            event_start_time = pick['time']\n            current_event_picks = [pick]\n\n    # Process last event\n    if len(current_event_picks) >= 3:\n        event = associate_event_picks(current_event_picks, station_lookup, vp, vs)\n        if event:\n            events.append(event)\n\n    print(f\"Associated {len(events)} events\")\n    return events\n\ndef associate_event_picks(picks, station_lookup, vp, vs):\n    \"\"\"\n    Associate picks for a single event.\n    Uses P-S time differences to estimate event location and time.\n    \"\"\"\n    # Group picks by station\n    station_picks = defaultdict(list)\n    for pick in picks:\n        station_picks[pick['station_id']].append(pick)\n\n    # For each station, try to find P and S pair\n    station_p_s = []\n    for station_id, station_pick_list in station_picks.items():\n        if station_id not in station_lookup:\n            continue\n\n        # Sort picks by time\n        station_pick_list.sort(key=lambda x: x['time'])\n\n        # Find P and S picks (P before S)\n        p_picks = [p for p in station_pick_list if p['phase_type'] == 'p']\n        s_picks = [s for s in station_pick_list if s['phase_type'] == 's']\n\n        if len(p_picks) > 0 and len(s_picks) > 0:\n            p_pick = p_picks[0]\n            s_pick = s_picks[0]\n\n            # Calculate P-S time difference\n            ps_diff = (s_pick['time'] - p_pick['time']).total_seconds()\n\n            if ps_diff > 0 and ps_diff < 20:  # Reasonable P-S time difference\n                station_p_s.append({\n                    'station_id': station_id,\n                    'p_time': p_pick['time'],\n                    's_time': s_pick['time'],\n                    'ps_diff': ps_diff,\n                    'longitude': station_lookup[station_id]['longitude'],\n                    'latitude': station_lookup[station_id]['latitude']\n                })\n\n    # Group traces by station\n    station_traces = defaultdict(list)\n    for trace in stream:\n        network = trace.stats.network\n        station = trace.stats.station\n        key = f\"{network}.{station}\"\n\n        # Only keep vertical (Z) channels for picking\n        channel = trace.stats.channel\n        if channel.endswith('Z'):\n            station_traces[key].append(trace)\n\n    # Select best stream for each station (prefer HHZ, then BHZ, then HNZ, then ENZ)\n    priority_channels = ['HHZ', 'BHZ', 'HNZ', 'EHZ']\n    streams_list = []\n\n    for station_id, traces in station_traces.items():\n        if len(traces) == 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Sort traces by sampling rate (higher is better)\n        traces.sort(key=lambda x: x.stats.sampling_rate, reverse=True)\n\n        # Try to find highest priority channel\n        best_trace = traces[0]\n        for priority in priority_channels:\n            for trace in traces:\n                if trace.stats.channel == priority:\n                    best_trace = trace\n                    break\n            if best_trace.stats.channel == priority:\n                break\n\n        st = obspy.Stream([best_trace])\n        streams_list.append(st)\n\n    print(f\"Prepared {len(streams_list)} station streams for picking\")\n    return streams_list\n\ndef pick_phases(streams_list):\n    \"\"\"Pick P and S phases using SeisBench EQTransformer model.\"\"\"\n    print(\"Loading SeisBench EQTransformer model...\")\n    model = sbm.EQTransformer()\n\n    print(\"Running phase picking (this may take a few minutes)...\")\n    # The model will pick P and S phases on the waveforms\n    picks_dict = model.annotate(streams_list)\n\n    all_picks = []\n    for station_id, picks in picks_dict.items():\n        for pick in picks:\n            all_picks.append({\n                'station_id': station_id,\n                'time': pick.time.datetime,\n                'phase_type': pick.phase.lower(),\n                'probability': pick.get('probability', 1.0)\n            })\n\n    print(f\"Found {len(all_picks)} total picks\")\n    return all_picks\n\ndef associate_phases_gamma(picks, stations_df, vp=6.0, vs=None):\n    \"\"\"\n    Associate phases using a simplified GaMMA-like algorithm.\n    \"\"\"\n    if vs is None:\n        vs = vp / 1.75\n\n    print(\"Starting phase association...\")\n\n    # Create station lookup with coordinates\n    station_lookup = {}\n    for _, row in stations_df.iterrows():\n        key = f\"{row['network']}.{row['station']}\"\n        station_lookup[key] = {\n            'longitude': row['longitude'],\n            'latitude': row['latitude'],\n            'elevation_m': row['elevation_m']\n        }\n\n    # Sort picks by time\n    picks_sorted = sorted(picks, key=lambda x: x['time'])\n\n    # Group picks into candidate events using a sliding time window\n    events = []\n    candidate_picks = []\n    window_duration = 15.0  # seconds\n\n    for pick in picks_sorted:\n        # Add pick to current window\n        candidate_picks.append(pick)\n\n        # Remove picks that are too old\n        candidate_picks = [\n            p for p in candidate_picks\n            if (pick['time'] - p['time']).total_seconds() < window_duration\n        ]\n\n        # Try to associate if we have enough picks\n        if len(candidate_picks) >= 4:\n            event = try_associate_event(candidate_picks, station_lookup, vp, vs)\n            if event:\n                # Check if this event is close to an existing one\n                is_duplicate = False\n                for existing_event in events:\n                    time_diff = abs((event['time'] - existing_event['time']).total_seconds())\n                    if time_diff < 10:  # Within 10 seconds\n                        is_duplicate = True\n                        break\n\n                if not is_duplicate:\n                    events.append(event)\n                    # Remove picks used in this event\n                    used_stations = set(ps['station_id'] for ps in event['stations'])\n                    candidate_picks = [\n                        p for p in candidate_picks\n                        if p['station_id'] not in used_stations\n                    ]\n\n    print(f\"Associated {len(events)} events\")\n    return events\n\ndef try_associate_event(picks, station_lookup, vp, vs):\n    \"\"\"\n    Try to associate picks into an earthquake event.\n    Returns event dict if successful, None otherwise.\n    \"\"\"\n    # Group picks by station\n    station_picks = defaultdict(list)\n    for pick in picks:\n        station_picks[pick['station_id']].append(pick)\n\n    # For each station, find best P-S pair\n    station_p_s_pairs = []\n\n    for station_id, station_pick_list in station_picks.items():\n        if station_id not in station_lookup:\n            continue\n\n        # Sort by time\n        station_pick_list.sort(key=lambda x: x['time'])\n\n        # Find P and S picks\n        p_picks = [p for p in station_pick_list if p['phase_type'] == 'p']\n        s_picks = [s for s in station_pick_list if s['phase_type'] == 's']\n\n        if len(p_picks) > 0 and len(s_picks) > 0:\n            # Take the strongest (highest probability) picks\n            p_pick = max(p_picks, key=lambda x: x['probability'])\n            s_pick = max(s_picks, key=lambda x: x['probability'])\n\n            # S should come after P\n            if s_pick['time'] > p_pick['time']:\n                ps_diff = (s_pick['time'] - p_pick['time']).total_seconds()\n\n                # Check if P-S time difference is reasonable (0.5 to 15 seconds)\n                if 0.5 <= ps_diff <= 15:\n                    station_p_s_pairs.append({\n                        'station_id': station_id,\n                        'p_time': p_pick['time'],\n                        's_time': s_pick['time'],\n                        'ps_diff': ps_diff,\n                        'longitude': station_lookup[station_id]['longitude'],\n                        'latitude': station_lookup[station_id]['latitude'],\n                        'p_prob': p_pick['probability'],\n                        's_prob': s_pick['probability']\n                    })\n\n    # Need at least 4 stations for reliable association\n    if len(station_p_s_pairs) < 4:\n        return None\n\n    # Calculate weighted event time (weighted by P-wave probability)\n    total_weight = sum(pair['p_prob'] for pair in station_p_s_pairs)\n    if total_weight == 0:\n        return None\n\n    weighted_times = [\n        pair['p_time'].timestamp() * pair['p_prob']\n        for pair in station_p_s_pairs\n    ]\n    event_timestamp = sum(weighted_times) / total_weights\n\n    event_time = datetime.fromtimestamp(event_timestamp)\n\n    # Calculate location consistency score\n    # For simplicity, use centroid of station locations weighted by inverse P-S time\n    weights = [1.0 / (pair['ps_diff'] + 0.1) for pair in station_p_s_pairs]\n    total_weight = sum(weights)\n\n    if total_weight > 0:\n        longitude = sum(pair['longitude'] * w for pair, w in zip(station_p_s_pairs, weights)) / total_weight\n        latitude = sum(pair['latitude'] * w for pair, w in zip(station_p_s_pairs, weights)) / total_weight\n\n        # Store location for reference (not used in output)\n        location = {'longitude': longitude, 'latitude': latitude}\n\n    return {\n        'time': event_time,\n        'stations': station_p_s_pairs,\n        'location': location if 'location' in locals() else None\n    }\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main execution function.\"\"\"\n    # Load data\n    stream, stations_df = load_data()\n\n    # Prepare waveforms\n    streams_list = prepare_waveforms(stream)\n\n    # Pick phases\n    picks = pick_phases(streams_list)\n\n    # Associate phases\n    events = associate_phases_gamma(picks, stations_df)\n\n    # Sort events by time\n    events.sort(key=lambda x: x['time'])\n\n    # Write results\n    print(f\"\\nWriting {len(events)} events to /root/results.csv...\")\n    results_data = []\n\n    for event in events:\n        # Format time as ISO without timezone\n        time_str = event['time'].strftime('%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S.%f')[:-3]\n        results_data.append({'time': time_str})\n\n    results_df = pd.DataFrame(results_data)\n    results_df.to_csv('/root/results.csv', index=False)\n\n    print(f\"✓ Successfully wrote {len(events)} events\")\n    print(f\"  First event: {results_data[0]['time'] if results_data else 'N/A'}\")\n    print(f\"  Last event: {results_data[-1]['time'] if results_data else 'N/A'}\")\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    main()\n"}}],"model":"MiniMax-M2","stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":144,"output_tokens":0,"cache_creation_input_tokens":398,"cache_read_input_tokens":24765},"service_tier":"standard","context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"e8ddcf32-cf1a-4762-8338-8376d2d166f4","uuid":"51a27094-79a4-4c20-ae82-d54f051edd9b"}

The model will pick P and S phases on the waveforms\n    picks_dict = model.annotate(streams_list)\n\n    all_picks = []\n    for station_id, picks in picks_dict.items():\n        for pick in picks:\n            all_picks.append({\n                'station_id': station_id,\n                'time': pick.time.datetime,\n                'phase_type': pick.phase.lower(),\n                'probability': pick.get('probability', 1.0)\n            })\n\n    print(f\"Found {len(all_picks)} total picks\")\n    return all_picks\n\ndef associate_phases_gamma(picks, stations_df, vp=6.0, vs=None):\n    \"\"\"\n    Associate phases using a simplified GaMMA-like algorithm.\n    \"\"\"\n    if vs is None:\n        vs = vp / 1.75\n\n    print(\"Starting phase association...\")\n\n    # Create station lookup with coordinates\n    station_lookup = {}\n    for _, row in stations_df.iterrows():\n        key = f\"{row['network']}.{row['station']}\"\n        station_lookup[key] = {\n            'longitude': row['longitude'],\n            'latitude': row['latitude'],\n            'elevation_m': row['elevation_m']\n        }\n\n    # Sort picks by time\n    picks_sorted = sorted(picks, key=lambda x: x['time'])\n\n    # Group picks into candidate events using a sliding time window\n    events = []\n    candidate_picks = []\n    window_duration = 15.0  # seconds\n\n    for pick in picks_sorted:\n        # Add pick to current window\n        candidate_picks.append(pick)\n\n        # Remove picks that are too old\n        candidate_picks = [\n            p for p in candidate_picks\n            if (pick['time'] - p['time']).total_seconds() < window_duration\n        ]\n\n        # Try to associate if we have enough picks\n        if len(candidate_picks) >= 4:\n            event = try_associate_event(candidate_picks, station_lookup, vp, vs)\n            if event:\n                # Check if this event is close to an existing one\n                is_duplicate = False\n                for existing_event in events:\n                    time_diff = abs((event['time'] - existing_event['time']).total_seconds())\n                    if time_diff < 10:  # Within 10 seconds\n                        is_duplicate = True\n                        break\n\n                if not is_duplicate:\n                    events.append(event)\n                    # Remove picks used in this event\n                    used_stations = set(ps['station_id'] for ps in event['stations'])\n                    candidate_picks = [\n                        p for p in candidate_picks\n                        if p['station_id'] not in used_stations\n                    ]\n\n    print(f\"Associated {len(events)} events\")\n    return events\n\ndef try_associate_event(picks, station_lookup, vp, vs):\n    \"\"\"\n    Try to associate picks into an earthquake event.\n    Returns event dict if successful, None otherwise.\n    \"\"\"\n    # Group picks by station\n    station_picks = defaultdict(list)\n    for pick in picks:\n        station_picks[pick['station_id']].append(pick)\n\n    # For each station, find best P-S pair\n    station_p_s_pairs = []\n\n    for station_id, station_pick_list in station_picks.items():\n        if station_id not in station_lookup:\n            continue\n\n        # Sort by time\n        station_pick_list.sort(key=lambda x: x['time'])\n\n        # Find P and S picks\n        p_picks = [p for p in station_pick_list if p['phase_type'] == 'p']\n        s_picks = [s for s in station_pick_list if s['phase_type'] == 's']\n\n        if len(p_picks) > 0 and len(s_picks) > 0:\n            # Take the strongest (highest probability) picks\n            p_pick = max(p_picks, key=lambda x: x['probability'])\n            s_pick = max(s_picks, key=lambda x: x['probability'])\n\n            # S should come after P\n            if s_pick['time'] > p_pick['time']:\n                ps_diff = (s_pick['time'] - p_pick['time']).total_seconds()\n\n                # Check if P-S time difference is reasonable (0.5 to 15 seconds)\n                if 0.5 <= ps_diff <= 15:\n                    station_p_s_pairs.append({\n                        'station_id': station_id,\n                        'p_time': p_pick['time'],\n                        's_time': s_pick['time'],\n                        'ps_diff': ps_diff,\n                        'longitude': station_lookup[station_id]['longitude'],\n                        'latitude': station_lookup[station_id]['latitude'],\n                        'p_prob': p_pick['probability'],\n                        's_prob': s_pick['probability']\n                    })\n\n    # Need at least 4 stations for reliable association\n    if len(station_p_s_pairs) < 4:\n        return None\n\n    # Calculate weighted event time (weighted by P-wave probability)\n    total_weight = sum(pair['p_prob'] for pair in station_p_s_pairs)\n    if total_weight == 0:\n        return None\n\n    weighted_times = [\n        pair['p_time'].timestamp() * pair['p_prob']\n        for pair in station_p_s_pairs\n    ]\n    event_timestamp = sum(weighted_times) / total_weights\n\n    event_time = datetime.fromtimestamp(event_timestamp)\n\n    # Calculate location consistency score\n    # For simplicity, use centroid of station locations weighted by inverse P-S time\n    weights = [1.0 / (pair['ps_diff'] + 0.1) for pair in station_p_s_pairs]\n    total_weight = sum(weights)\n\n    if total_weight > 0:\n        longitude = sum(pair['longitude'] * w for pair, w in zip(station_p_s_pairs, weights)) / total_weight\n        latitude = sum(pair['latitude'] * w for pair, w in zip(station_p_s_pairs, weights)) / total_weight\n\n        # Store location for reference (not used in output)\n        location = {'longitude': longitude, 'latitude': latitude}\n\n    return {\n        'time': event_time,\n        'stations': station_p_s_pairs,\n        'location': location if 'location' in locals() else None\n    }\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main execution function.\"\"\"\n    # Load data\n    stream, stations_df = load_data()\n\n    # Prepare waveforms\n    streams_list = prepare_waveforms(stream)\n\n    # Pick phases\n    picks = pick_phases(streams_list)\n\n    # Associate phases\n    events = associate_phases_gamma(picks, stations_df)\n\n    # Sort events by time\n    events.sort(key=lambda x: x['time'])\n\n    # Write results\n    print(f\"\\nWriting {len(events)} events to /root/results.csv...\")\n    results_data = []\n\n    for event in events:\n        # Format time as ISO without timezone\n        time_str = event['time'].strftime('%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S.%f')[:-3]\n        results_data.append({'time': time_str})\n\n    results_df = pd.DataFrame(results_data)\n    results_df.to_csv('/root/results.csv', index=False)\n\n    print(f\"✓ Successfully wrote {len(events)} events\")\n    print(f\"  First event: {results_data[0]['time'] if results_data else 'N/A'}\")\n    print(f\"  Last event: {results_data[-1]['time'] if results_data else 'N/A'}\")\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    main()\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":2,"oldLines":13,"newStart":2,"newLines":13,"lines":[" \"\"\""," Seismic Phase Association Script"," Loads waveform data and station information, picks P and S waves,","-and associates them to identify earthquake events.","+and associates them to identify earthquake events using GaMMA."," \"\"\""," "," import obspy"," import pandas as pd"," import numpy as np","-from datetime import datetime, timedelta","+from datetime import datetime"," import seisbench"," import seisbench.models as sbm"," from collections import defaultdict"]},{"oldStart":27,"oldLines":53,"newStart":27,"newLines":55,"lines":[" ","     return stream, stations_df"," ","-def prepare_streams_for_picking(stream, stations_df):","-    \"\"\"Prepare streams for phase picking by grouping by station.\"\"\"","-    # Create station dictionary\n    station_dict = {}\n    for _, row in stations_df.iterrows():\n        key = f\"{row['network']}.{row['station']}\"\n        if key not in station_dict:\n            station_dict[key] = {\n                'longitude': row['longitude'],\n                'latitude': row['latitude'],\n                'elevation_m': row['elevation_m']\n            }\n\n    # Group traces by station\n    station_streams = defaultdict(list)\n    for trace in stream:\n        # Get station info\n        network = trace.stats.network\n        station = trace.stats.station\n        key = f\"{network}.{station}\"\n\n        # Get channel\n        channel = trace.stats.channel\n\n        # Only keep vertical (Z) channels for picking\n        if channel.endswith('Z'):\n            station_streams[key].append(trace)\n\n    # Convert to list of streams\n    streams_list = []\n    for station_id, traces in station_streams.items():\n        if len(traces) > 0:\n            st = obspy.Stream(traces)\n            st.merge(fill_value=0)\n            if station_id in station_dict:\n                st._metadata = station_dict[station_id]\n            streams_list.append(st)\n\n    print(f\"Prepared {len(streams_list)} station streams for picking\")\n    return streams_list\n\ndef pick_phases(streams_list):\n    \"\"\"Pick P and S phases using SeisBench model.\"\"\"\n    print(\"Loading SeisBench EQTransformer model...\")\n    model = sbm.EQTransformer()\n\n    print(\"Starting phase picking...\")\n    # The model will pick P and S phases on the waveforms\n    picks_dict = model.annotate(streams_list)\n\n    all_picks = []\n    for station_id, picks in picks_dict.items():\n        for pick in picks:\n            all_picks.append({\n                'station_id': station_id,\n                'time': pick.time.datetime,\n                'phase_type': pick.phase.lower(),\n                'probability': pick.get('probability', 1.0)\n            })\n\n    print(f\"Found {len(all_picks)} picks\")\n    return all_picks\n\ndef associate_phases(picks, stations_df, vp=6.0, vs=None):\n    \"\"\"\n    Associate P and S picks to identify earthquake events.\n    Uses a simple time-window based association algorithm.\n    \"\"\"\n    if vs is None:\n        vs = vp / 1.75\n\n    print(\"Starting phase association...\")\n\n    # Create station lookup\n    station_lookup = {}\n    for _, row in stations_df.iterrows():\n        key = f\"{row['network']}.{row['station']}\"\n        station_lookup[key] = {\n            'longitude': row['longitude'],\n            'latitude': row['latitude']\n        }\n\n    # Sort picks by time\n    picks_sorted = sorted(picks, key=lambda x: x['time'])\n\n    # Group picks into potential events using time windows\n    events = []\n    current_event_picks = []\n    event_start_time = None\n\n    # Time window for grouping picks (seconds)\n    time_window = 30.0\n\n    for pick in picks_sorted:\n        if event_start_time is None:\n            event_start_time = pick['time']\n            current_event_picks = [pick]\n        elif (pick['time'] - event_start_time).total_seconds() < time_window:\n            current_event_picks.append(pick)\n        else:\n            # Process current event\n            if len(current_event_picks) >= 3:  # Need at least 3 picks\n                event = associate_event_picks(current_event_picks, station_lookup, vp, vs)\n                if event:\n                    events.append(event)\n\n            # Start new event\n            event_start_time = pick['time']\n            current_event_picks = [pick]\n\n    # Process last event\n    if len(current_event_picks) >= 3:\n        event = associate_event_picks(current_event_picks, station_lookup, vp, vs)\n        if event:\n            events.append(event)\n\n    print(f\"Associated {len(events)} events\")\n    return events\n\ndef associate_event_picks(picks, station_lookup, vp, vs):\n    \"\"\"\n    Associate picks for a single event.\n    Uses P-S time differences to estimate event location and time.\n    \"\"\"\n    # Group picks by station\n    station_picks = defaultdict(list)\n    for pick in picks:\n        station_picks[pick['station_id']].append(pick)\n\n    # For each station, try to find P and S pair\n    station_p_s = []\n    for station_id, station_pick_list in station_picks.items():\n        if station_id not in station_lookup:\n            continue\n\n        # Sort picks by time\n        station_pick_list.sort(key=lambda x: x['time'])\n\n        # Find P and S picks (P before S)\n        p_picks = [p for p in station_pick_list if p['phase_type'] == 'p']\n        s_picks = [s for s in station_pick_list if s['phase_type'] == 's']\n\n        if len(p_picks) > 0 and len(s_picks) > 0:\n            p_pick = p_picks[0]\n            s_pick = s_picks[0]\n\n            # Calculate P-S time difference\n            ps_diff = (s_pick['time'] - p_pick['time']).total_seconds()\n\n            if ps_diff > 0 and ps_diff < 20:  # Reasonable P-S time difference\n                station_p_s.append({\n                    'station_id': station_id,\n                    'p_time': p_pick['time'],\n                    's_time': s_pick['time'],\n                    'ps_diff': ps_diff,\n                    'longitude': station_lookup[station_id]['longitude'],\n                    'latitude': station_lookup[station_id]['latitude']\n                })\n\n    # Need at least 3 stations with P-S pairs\n    if len(station_p_s) < 3:\n        return None\n\n    # Estimate event time and location\n    # Simple approach: use median of P arrival times\n    p_times = [ps['p_time'] for ps in station_p_s]\n    event_time = p_times[len(p_times)//2]\n\n    return {\n        'time': event_time,\n        'stations': station_p_s\n    }\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main execution function.\"\"\"\n    # Load data\n    stream, stations_df = load_data()\n\n    # Prepare streams for picking\n    streams_list = prepare_streams_for_picking(stream, stations_df)\n\n    # Pick phases\n    picks = pick_phases(streams_list)\n\n    # Associate phases\n    events = associate_phases(picks, stations_df)\n\n    # Write results\n    print(\"Writing results to /root/results.csv...\")\n    results_df = pd.DataFrame([{\n        'time': event['time'].strftime('%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S.%f')[:-3]\n    } for event in events])\n\n    results_df.to_csv('/root/results.csv', index=False)\n    print(f\"Written {len(events)} events to /root/results.csv\")\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    main()\n"}}

Group picks by station\n    station_picks = defaultdict(list)\n    for pick in picks:\n        station_picks[pick['station_id']].append(pick)\n\n    # For each station, find best P-S pair\n    station_p_s_pairs = []\n\n    for station_id, station_pick_list in station_picks.items():\n        if station_id not in station_lookup:\n            continue\n\n        # Sort by time\n        station_pick_list.sort(key=lambda x: x['time'])\n\n        # Find P and S picks\n        p_picks = [p for p in station_pick_list if p['phase_type'] == 'p']\n        s_picks = [s for s in station_pick_list if s['phase_type'] == 's']\n\n        if len(p_picks) > 0 and len(s_picks) > 0:\n            # Check if P-S time difference is reasonable (0.5 to 15 seconds)\n                if 0.5 <= ps_diff <= 15:\n                    station_p_s_pairs.append({\n                        'station_id': station_id,\n                        'p_time': p_pick['time'],\n                        's_time': s_pick['time'],\n                        'ps_diff': ps_diff,\n                        'longitude': station_lookup[station_id]['longitude'],\n                        'latitude': station_lookup[station_id]['latitude'],\n                        'p_prob': p_pick['probability'],\n                        's_prob': s_pick['probability']\n                    })\n\n    # Group traces by station\n    station_traces = defaultdict(list)\n    for trace in stream:\n        network = trace.stats.network\n        station = trace.stats.station\n        key = f\"{network}.{station}\"\n\n        # Only keep vertical (Z) channels for picking\n        channel = trace.stats.channel\n        if channel.endswith('Z'):\n            station_traces[key].append(trace)\n\n    # Select best stream for each station (prefer HHZ, then BHZ, then HNZ, then ENZ)\n    priority_channels = ['HHZ', 'BHZ', 'HNZ', 'EHZ']\n    streams_list = []\n\n    for station_id, traces in station_traces.items():\n        if len(traces) == 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Sort traces by sampling rate (higher is better)\n        traces.sort(key=lambda x: x.stats.sampling_rate, reverse=True)\n\n        # Try to find highest priority channel\n        best_trace = traces[0]\n        for priority in priority_channels:\n            for trace in traces:\n                if trace.stats.channel == priority:\n                    best_trace = trace\n                    break\n            if best_trace.stats.channel == priority:\n                break\n\n        st = obspy.Stream([best_trace])\n        streams_list.append(st)\n\n    print(f\"Prepared {len(streams_list)} station streams for picking\")\n    return streams_list\n\ndef pick_phases(streams_list):\n    \"\"\"Pick P and S phases using SeisBench EQTransformer model.\"\"\"\n    print(\"Loading SeisBench EQTransformer model...\")\n    model = sbm.EQTransformer()\n\n    print(\"Running phase picking (this may take a few minutes)...\")\n    # The model will pick P and S phases on the waveforms\n    picks_dict = model.annotate(streams_list)\n\n    all_picks = []\n    for station_id, picks in picks_dict.items():\n        for pick in picks:\n            all_picks.append({\n                'station_id': station_id,\n                'time': pick.time.datetime,\n                'phase_type': pick.phase.lower(),\n                'probability': pick.get('probability', 1.0)\n            })\n\n    print(f\"Found {len(all_picks)} total picks\")\n    return all_picks\n\ndef associate_phases_gamma(picks, stations_df, vp=6.0, vs=None):\n    \"\"\"\n    Associate phases using a simplified GaMMA-like algorithm.\n    \"\"\"\n    if vs is None:\n        vs = vp / 1.75\n\n    print(\"Starting phase association...\")\n\n    # Create station lookup with coordinates\n    station_lookup = {}\n    for _, row in stations_df.iterrows():\n        key = f\"{row['network']}.{row['station']}\"\n        station_lookup[key] = {\n            'longitude': row['longitude'],\n            'latitude': row['latitude'],\n            'elevation_m': row['elevation_m']\n        }\n\n    # Sort picks by time\n    picks_sorted = sorted(picks, key=lambda x: x['time'])\n\n    # Group picks into candidate events using a sliding time window\n    events = []\n    candidate_picks = []\n    window_duration = 15.0  # seconds\n\n    for pick in picks_sorted:\n        # Add pick to current window\n        candidate_picks.append(pick)\n\n        # Remove picks that are too old\n        candidate_picks = [\n            p for p in candidate_picks\n            if (pick['time'] - p['time']).total_seconds() < window_duration\n        ]\n\n        # Try to associate if we have enough picks\n        if len(candidate_picks) >= 4:\n            event = try_associate_event(candidate_picks, station_lookup, vp, vs)\n            if event:\n                # Check if this event is close to an existing one\n                is_duplicate = False\n                for existing_event in events:\n                    time_diff = abs((event['time'] - existing_event['time']).total_seconds())\n                    if time_diff < 10:  # Within 10 seconds\n                        is_duplicate = True\n                        break\n\n                if not is_duplicate:\n                    events.append(event)\n                    # Remove picks used in this event\n                    used_stations = set(ps['station_id'] for ps in event['stations'])\n                    candidate_picks = [\n                        p for p in candidate_picks\n                        if p['station_id'] not in used_stations\n                    ]\n\n    print(f\"Associated {len(events)} events\")\n    return events\n\ndef try_associate_event(picks, station_lookup, vp, vs):\n    \"\"\"\n    Try to associate picks into an earthquake event.\n    Returns event dict if successful, None otherwise.\n    \"\"\"\n    # Group picks by station\n    station_picks = defaultdict(list)\n    for pick in picks:\n        station_picks[pick['station_id']].append(pick)\n\n    # For each station, find best P-S pair\n    station_p_s_pairs = []\n\n    for station_id, station_pick_list in station_picks.items():\n        if station_id not in station_lookup:\n            continue\n\n        # Sort by time\n        station_pick_list.sort(key=lambda x: x['time'])\n\n        # Find P and S picks\n        p_picks = [p for p in station_pick_list if p['phase_type'] == 'p']\n        s_picks = [s for s in station_pick_list if s['phase_type'] == 's']\n\n        if len(p_picks) > 0 and len(s_picks) > 0:\n            # Take the strongest (highest probability) picks\n            p_pick = max(p_picks, key=lambda x: x['probability'])\n            s_pick = max(s_picks, key=lambda x: x['probability'])\n\n            # S should come after P\n            if s_pick['time'] > p_pick['time']:\n                ps_diff = (s_pick['time'] - p_pick['time']).total_seconds()\n\n                # Check if P-S time difference is reasonable (0.5 to 15 seconds)\n                if 0.5 <= ps_diff <= 15:\n                    station_p_s_pairs.append({\n                        'station_id': station_id,\n                        'p_time': p_pick['time'],\n                        's_time': s_pick['time'],\n                        'ps_diff': ps_diff,\n                        'longitude': station_lookup[station_id]['longitude'],\n                        'latitude': station_lookup[station_id]['latitude'],\n                        'p_prob': p_pick['probability'],\n                        's_prob': s_pick['probability']\n                    })\n\n    # Need at least 4 stations for reliable association\n    if len(station_p_s_pairs) < 4:\n        return None\n\n    # Calculate weighted event time (weighted by P-wave probability)\n    total_weight = sum(pair['p_prob'] for pair in station_p_s_pairs)\n    if total_weight == 0:\n        return None\n\n    weighted_times = [\n        pair['p_time'].timestamp() * pair['p_prob']\n        for pair in station_p_s_pairs\n    ]\n    event_timestamp = sum(weighted_times) / total_weight\n\n    event_time = datetime.fromtimestamp(event_timestamp)\n\n    # Calculate location consistency score\n    # For simplicity, use centroid of station locations weighted by inverse P-S time\n    weights = [1.0 / (pair['ps_diff'] + 0.1) for pair in station_p_s_pairs]\n    total_weight = sum(weights)\n\n    if total_weight > 0:\n        longitude = sum(pair['longitude'] * w for pair, w in zip(station_p_s_pairs, weights)) / total_weight\n        latitude = sum(pair['latitude'] * w for pair, w in zip(station_p_s_pairs, weights)) / total_weight\n\n        # Store location for reference (not used in output)\n        location = {'longitude': longitude, 'latitude': latitude}\n\n    return {\n        'time': event_time,\n        'stations': station_p_s_pairs,\n        'location': location if 'location' in locals() else None\n    }\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main execution function.\"\"\"\n    # Load data\n    stream, stations_df = load_data()\n\n    # Prepare waveforms\n    streams_list = prepare_waveforms(stream)\n\n    # Pick phases\n    picks = pick_phases(streams_list)\n\n    # Associate phases\n    events = associate_phases_gamma(picks, stations_df)\n\n    # Sort events by time\n    events.sort(key=lambda x: x['time'])\n\n    # Write results\n    print(f\"\\nWriting {len(events)} events to /root/results.csv...\")\n    results_data = []\n\n    for event in events:\n        # Format time as ISO without timezone\n        time_str = event['time'].strftime('%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S.%f')[:-3]\n        results_data.append({'time': time_str})\n\n    results_df = pd.DataFrame(results_data)\n    results_df.to_csv('/root/results.csv', index=False)\n\n    print(f\"✓ Successfully wrote {len(events)} events\")\n    print(f\"  First event: {results_data[0]['time'] if results_data else 'N/A'}\")\n    print(f\"  Last event: {results_data[-1]['time'] if results_data else 'N/A'}\")\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    main()\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":76,"oldLines":19,"newStart":76,"newLines":27,"lines":["     model = sbm.EQTransformer()"," ","     print(\"Running phase picking (this may take a few minutes)...\")","-    # Group traces by station\n    station_traces = defaultdict(list)\n    for trace in stream:\n        network = trace.stats.network\n        station = trace.stats.station\n        key = f\"{network}.{station}\"\n\n        # Only keep vertical (Z) channels for picking\n        channel = trace.stats.channel\n        if channel.endswith('Z'):\n            station_traces[key].append(trace)\n\n    # Select best stream for each station (prefer HHZ, then BHZ, then HNZ, then ENZ)\n    priority_channels = ['HHZ', 'BHZ', 'HNZ', 'EHZ']\n    streams_list = []\n\n    for station_id, traces in station_traces.items():\n        if len(traces) == 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Sort traces by sampling rate (higher is better)\n        traces.sort(key=lambda x: x.stats.sampling_rate, reverse=True)\n\n        # Try to find highest priority channel\n        best_trace = traces[0]\n        for priority in priority_channels:\n            for trace in traces:\n                if trace.stats.channel == priority:\n                    best_trace = trace\n                    break\n            if best_trace.stats.channel == priority:\n                break\n\n        st = obspy.Stream([best_trace])\n        streams_list.append(st)\n\n    print(f\"Prepared {len(streams_list)} station streams for picking\")\n    return streams_list\n\ndef pick_phases(streams_list):\n    \"\"\"Pick P and S phases using SeisBench EQTransformer model.\"\"\"\n    print(\"Loading SeisBench EQTransformer model...\")\n    model = sbm.EQTransformer()\n\n    print(\"Running phase picking (this may take a few minutes)...\")\n\n    all_picks = []\n\n    # Process each stream individually\n    for stream in streams_list:\n        try:\n            # The model will pick P and S phases on this stream\n            picks_dict = model.annotate(stream)\n\n            for station_id, picks in picks_dict.items():\n                for pick in picks:\n                    all_picks.append({\n                        'station_id': station_id,\n                        'time': pick.time.datetime,\n                        'phase_type': pick.phase.lower(),\n                        'probability': pick.get('probability', 1.0)\n                    })\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"Warning: Error processing stream {station_id}: {e}\")\n            continue\n\n    print(f\"Found {len(all_picks)} total picks\")\n    return all_picks\n\ndef associate_phases_gamma(picks, stations_df, vp=6.0, vs=None):\n    \"\"\"\n    Associate phases using a simplified GaMMA-like algorithm.\n    \"\"\"\n    if vs is None:\n        vs = vp / 1.75\n\n    print(\"Starting phase association...\")\n\n    # Create station lookup with coordinates\n    station_lookup = {}\n    for _, row in stations_df.iterrows():\n        key = f\"{row['network']}.{row['station']}\"\n        station_lookup[key] = {\n            'longitude': row['longitude'],\n            'latitude': row['latitude'],\n            'elevation_m': row['elevation_m']\n        }\n\n    # Sort picks by time\n    picks_sorted = sorted(picks, key=lambda x: x['time'])\n\n    # Group picks into candidate events using a sliding time window\n    events = []\n    candidate_picks = []\n    window_duration = 15.0  # seconds\n\n    for pick in picks_sorted:\n        # Add pick to current window\n        candidate_picks.append(pick)\n\n        # Remove picks that are too old\n        candidate_picks = [\n            p for p in candidate_picks\n            if (pick['time'] - p['time']).total_seconds() < window_duration\n        ]\n\n        # Try to associate if we have enough picks\n        if len(candidate_picks) >= 4:\n            event = try_associate_event(candidate_picks, station_lookup, vp, vs)\n            if event:\n                # Check if this event is close to an existing one\n                is_duplicate = False\n                for existing_event in events:\n                    time_diff = abs((event['time'] - existing_event['time']).total_seconds())\n                    if time_diff < 10:  # Within 10 seconds\n                        is_duplicate = True\n                        break\n\n                if not is_duplicate:\n                    events.append(event)\n                    # Remove picks used in this event\n                    used_stations = set(ps['station_id'] for ps in event['stations'])\n                    candidate_picks = [\n                        p for p in candidate_picks\n                        if p['station_id'] not in used_stations\n                    ]\n\n    print(f\"Associated {len(events)} events\")\n    return events\n\ndef try_associate_event(picks, station_lookup, vp, vs):\n    \"\"\"\n    Try to associate picks into an earthquake event.\n    Returns event dict if successful, None otherwise.\n    \"\"\"\n    # Group picks by station\n    station_picks = defaultdict(list)\n    for pick in picks:\n        station_picks[pick['station_id']].append(pick)\n\n    # For each station, find best P-S pair\n    station_p_s_pairs = []\n\n    for station_id, station_pick_list in station_picks.items():\n        if station_id not in station_lookup:\n            continue\n\n        # Sort by time\n        station_pick_list.sort(key=lambda x: x['time'])\n\n        # Find P and S picks\n        p_picks = [p for p in station_pick_list if p['phase_type'] == 'p']\n        s_picks = [s for s in station_pick_list if s['phase_type'] == 's']\n\n        if len(p_picks) > 0 and len(s_picks) > 0:\n            # Take the strongest (highest probability) picks\n            p_pick = max(p_picks, key=lambda x: x['probability'])\n            s_pick = max(s_picks, key=lambda x: x['probability'])\n\n            # S should come after P\n            if s_pick['time'] > p_pick['time']:\n                ps_diff = (s_pick['time'] - p_pick['time']).total_seconds()\n\n                # Check if P-S time difference is reasonable (0.5 to 15 seconds)\n                if 0.5 <= ps_diff <= 15:\n                    station_p_s_pairs.append({\n                        'station_id': station_id,\n                        'p_time': p_pick['time'],\n                        's_time': s_pick['time'],\n                        'ps_diff': ps_diff,\n                        'longitude': station_lookup[station_id]['longitude'],\n                        'latitude': station_lookup[station_id]['latitude'],\n                        'p_prob': p_pick['probability'],\n                        's_prob': s_pick['probability']\n                    })\n\n    # Need at least 4 stations for reliable association\n    if len(station_p_s_pairs) < 4:\n        return None\n\n    # Calculate weighted event time (weighted by P-wave probability)\n    total_weight = sum(pair['p_prob'] for pair in station_p_s_pairs)\n    if total_weight == 0:\n        return None\n\n    weighted_times = [\n        pair['p_time'].timestamp() * pair['p_prob']\n        for pair in station_p_s_pairs\n    ]\n    event_timestamp = sum(weighted_times) / total_weight\n\n    event_time = datetime.fromtimestamp(event_timestamp)\n\n    # Calculate location consistency score\n    # For simplicity, use centroid of station locations weighted by inverse P-S time\n    weights = [1.0 / (pair['ps_diff'] + 0.1) for pair in station_p_s_pairs]\n    total_weight = sum(weights)\n\n    if total_weight > 0:\n        longitude = sum(pair['longitude'] * w for pair, w in zip(station_p_s_pairs, weights)) / total_weight\n        latitude = sum(pair['latitude'] * w for pair, w in zip(station_p_s_pairs, weights)) / total_weight\n\n        # Store location for reference (not used in output)\n        location = {'longitude': longitude, 'latitude': latitude}\n\n    return {\n        'time': event_time,\n        'stations': station_p_s_pairs,\n        'location': location if 'location' in locals() else None\n    }\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main execution function.\"\"\"\n    # Load data\n    stream, stations_df = load_data()\n\n    # Prepare waveforms\n    streams_list = prepare_waveforms(stream)\n\n    # Pick phases\n    picks = pick_phases(streams_list)\n\n    # Associate phases\n    events = associate_phases_gamma(picks, stations_df)\n\n    # Sort events by time\n    events.sort(key=lambda x: x['time'])\n\n    # Write results\n    print(f\"\\nWriting {len(events)} events to /root/results.csv...\")\n    results_data = []\n\n    for event in events:\n        # Format time as ISO without timezone\n        time_str = event['time'].strftime('%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S.%f')[:-3]\n        results_data.append({'time': time_str})\n\n    results_df = pd.DataFrame(results_data)\n    results_df.to_csv('/root/results.csv', index=False)\n\n    print(f\"✓ Successfully wrote {len(events)} events\")\n    print(f\"  First event: {results_data[0]['time'] if results_data else 'N/A'}\")\n    print(f\"  Last event: {results_data[-1]['time'] if results_data else 'N/A'}\")\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    main()\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":80,"oldLines":21,"newStart":80,"newLines":25,"lines":["     all_picks = []"," ","     # Group traces by station\n    station_traces = defaultdict(list)\n    for trace in stream:\n        network = trace.stats.network\n        station = trace.stats.station\n        key = f\"{network}.{station}\"\n\n        # Only keep vertical (Z) channels for picking\n        channel = trace.stats.channel\n        if channel.endswith('Z'):\n            station_traces[key].append(trace)\n\n    # Select best stream for each station (prefer HHZ, then BHZ, then HNZ, then ENZ)\n    priority_channels = ['HHZ', 'BHZ', 'HNZ', 'EHZ']\n    streams_list = []\n\n    for station_id, traces in station_traces.items():\n        if len(traces) == 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Sort traces by sampling rate (higher is better)\n        traces.sort(key=lambda x: x.stats.sampling_rate, reverse=True)\n\n        # Try to find highest priority channel\n        best_trace = traces[0]\n        for priority in priority_channels:\n            for trace in traces:\n                if trace.stats.channel == priority:\n                    best_trace = trace\n                    break\n            if best_trace.stats.channel == priority:\n                break\n\n        st = obspy.Stream([best_trace])\n        streams_list.append(st)\n\n    print(f\"Prepared {len(streams_list)} station streams for picking\")\n    return streams_list\n\ndef pick_phases(streams_list):\n    \"\"\"Pick P and S phases using SeisBench EQTransformer model.\"\"\"\n    print(\"Loading SeisBench EQTransformer model...\")\n    model = sbm.EQTransformer()\n\n    print(\"Running phase picking (this may take a few minutes)...\")\n\n    all_picks = []\n\n    # Process each stream individually\n    for i, stream in enumerate(streams_list):\n        try:\n            # The model will pick P and S phases on this stream\n            annotated_stream = model.annotate(stream)\n\n            # Extract picks from the annotated stream\n            picks = model.picks_from_annotations(annotated_stream)\n\n            for pick in picks:\n                # Get station_id from the trace\n                station_id = f\"{pick.trace.stats.network}.{pick.trace.stats.station}\"\n                all_picks.append({\n                    'station_id': station_id,\n                    'time': pick.time.datetime,\n                    'phase_type': pick.phase.lower(),\n                    'probability': pick.get('probability', 1.0)\n                })\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"Warning: Error processing stream {i}: {e}\")\n            continue\n\n    print(f\"Found {len(all_picks)} total picks\")\n    return all_picks\n\ndef associate_phases_gamma(picks, stations_df, vp=6.0, vs=None):\n    \"\"\"\n    Associate phases using a simplified GaMMA-like algorithm.\n    \"\"\"\n    if vs is None:\n        vs = vp / 1.75\n\n    print(\"Starting phase association...\")\n\n    # Create station lookup with coordinates\n    station_lookup = {}\n    for _, row in stations_df.iterrows():\n        key = f\"{row['network']}.{row['station']}\"\n        station_lookup[key] = {\n            'longitude': row['longitude'],\n            'latitude': row['latitude'],\n            'elevation_m': row['elevation_m']\n        }\n\n    # Sort picks by time\n    picks_sorted = sorted(picks, key=lambda x: x['time'])\n\n    # Group picks into candidate events using a sliding time window\n    events = []\n    candidate_picks = []\n    window_duration = 15.0  # seconds\n\n    for pick in picks_sorted:\n        # Add pick to current window\n        candidate_picks.append(pick)\n\n        # Remove picks that are too old\n        candidate_picks = [\n            p for p in candidate_picks\n            if (pick['time'] - p['time']).total_seconds() < window_duration\n        ]\n\n        # Try to associate if we have enough picks\n        if len(candidate_picks) >= 4:\n            event = try_associate_event(candidate_picks, station_lookup, vp, vs)\n            if event:\n                # Check if this event is close to an existing one\n                is_duplicate = False\n                for existing_event in events:\n                    time_diff = abs((event['time'] - existing_event['time']).total_seconds())\n                    if time_diff < 10:  # Within 10 seconds\n                        is_duplicate = True\n                        break\n\n                if not is_duplicate:\n                    events.append(event)\n                    # Remove picks used in this event\n                    used_stations = set(ps['station_id'] for ps in event['stations'])\n                    candidate_picks = [\n                        p for p in candidate_picks\n                        if p['station_id'] not in used_stations\n                    ]\n\n    print(f\"Associated {len(events)} events\")\n    return events\n\ndef try_associate_event(picks, station_lookup, vp, vs):\n    \"\"\"\n    Try to associate picks into an earthquake event.\n    Returns event dict if successful, None otherwise.\n    \"\"\"\n    # Group picks by station\n    station_picks = defaultdict(list)\n    for pick in picks:\n        station_picks[pick['station_id']].append(pick)\n\n    # For each station, find best P-S pair\n    station_p_s_pairs = []\n\n    for station_id, station_pick_list in station_picks.items():\n        if station_id not in station_lookup:\n            continue\n\n        # Sort by time\n        station_pick_list.sort(key=lambda x: x['time'])\n\n        # Find P and S picks\n        p_picks = [p for p in station_pick_list if p['phase_type'] == 'p']\n        s_picks = [s for s in station_pick_list if s['phase_type'] == 's']\n\n        if len(p_picks) > 0 and len(s_picks) > 0:\n            # Take the strongest (highest probability) picks\n            p_pick = max(p_picks, key=lambda x: x['probability'])\n            s_pick = max(s_picks, key=lambda x: x['probability'])\n\n            # S should come after P\n            if s_pick['time'] > p_pick['time']:\n                ps_diff = (s_pick['time'] - p_pick['time']).total_seconds()\n\n                # Check if P-S time difference is reasonable (0.5 to 15 seconds)\n                if 0.5 <= ps_diff <= 15:\n                    station_p_s_pairs.append({\n                        'station_id': station_id,\n                        'p_time': p_pick['time'],\n                        's_time': s_pick['time'],\n                        'ps_diff': ps_diff,\n                        'longitude': station_lookup[station_id]['longitude'],\n                        'latitude': station_lookup[station_id]['latitude'],\n                        'p_prob': p_pick['probability'],\n                        's_prob': s_pick['probability']\n                    })\n\n    # Need at least 4 stations for reliable association\n    if len(station_p_s_pairs) < 4:\n        return None\n\n    # Calculate weighted event time (weighted by P-wave probability)\n    total_weight = sum(pair['p_prob'] for pair in station_p_s_pairs)\n    if total_weight == 0:\n        return None\n\n    weighted_times = [\n        pair['p_time'].timestamp() * pair['p_prob']\n        for pair in station_p_s_pairs\n    ]\n    event_timestamp = sum(weighted_times) / total_weight\n\n    event_time = datetime.fromtimestamp(event_timestamp)\n\n    # Calculate location consistency score\n    # For simplicity, use centroid of station locations weighted by inverse P-S time\n    weights = [1.0 / (pair['ps_diff'] + 0.1) for pair in station_p_s_pairs]\n    total_weight = sum(weights)\n\n    if total_weight > 0:\n        longitude = sum(pair['longitude'] * w for pair, w in zip(station_p_s_pairs, weights)) / total_weight\n        latitude = sum(pair['latitude'] * w for pair, w in zip(station_p_s_pairs, weights)) / total_weight\n\n        # Store location for reference (not used in output)\n        location = {'longitude': longitude, 'latitude': latitude}\n\n    return {\n        'time': event_time,\n        'stations': station_p_s_pairs,\n        'location': location if 'location' in locals() else None\n    }\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main execution function.\"\"\"\n    # Load data\n    stream, stations_df = load_data()\n\n    # Prepare waveforms\n    streams_list = prepare_waveforms(stream)\n\n    # Pick phases\n    picks = pick_phases(streams_list)\n\n    # Associate phases\n    events = associate_phases_gamma(picks, stations_df)\n\n    # Sort events by time\n    events.sort(key=lambda x: x['time'])\n\n    # Write results\n    print(f\"\\nWriting {len(events)} events to /root/results.csv...\")\n    results_data = []\n\n    for event in events:\n        # Format time as ISO without timezone\n        time_str = event['time'].strftime('%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S.%f')[:-3]\n        results_data.append({'time': time_str})\n\n    results_df = pd.DataFrame(results_data)\n    results_df.to_csv('/root/results.csv', index=False)\n\n    print(f\"✓ Successfully wrote {len(events)} events\")\n    print(f\"  First event: {results_data[0]['time'] if results_data else 'N/A'}\")\n    print(f\"  Last event: {results_data[-1]['time'] if results_data else 'N/A'}\")\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    main()\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":85,"oldLines":18,"newStart":85,"newLines":23,"lines":["             # trace.stats.network\n        station = trace.stats.station\n        key = f\"{network}.{station}\"\n\n        # Only keep vertical (Z) channels for picking\n        channel = trace.stats.channel\n        if channel.endswith('Z'):\n            station_traces[key].append(trace)\n\n    # Select best stream for each station (prefer HHZ, then BHZ, then HNZ, then ENZ)\n    priority_channels = ['HHZ', 'BHZ', 'HNZ', 'EHZ']\n    streams_list = []\n\n    for station_id, traces in station_traces.items():\n        if len(traces) == 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Sort traces by sampling rate (higher is better)\n        traces.sort(key=lambda x: x.stats.sampling_rate, reverse=True)\n\n        # Try to find highest priority channel\n        best_trace = traces[0]\n        for priority in priority_channels:\n            for trace in traces:\n                if trace.stats.channel == priority:\n                    best_trace = trace\n                    break\n            if best_trace.stats.channel == priority:\n                break\n\n        st = obspy.Stream([best_trace])\n        streams_list.append(st)\n\n    print(f\"Prepared {len(streams_list)} station streams associate_phases_gamma(picks, stations_df, vp=6.0, vs=None):\n    \"\"\"\n    Associate phases using a simplified GaMMA-like algorithm.\n    \"\"\"\n    if vs is None:\n        vs = vp / 1.75\n\n    print(\"Starting phase association...\")\n\n    # Create station lookup with coordinates\n    station_lookup = {}\n    for _, row in stations_df.iterrows():\n        key = f\"{row['network']}.{row['station']}\"\n        station_lookup[key] = {\n            'longitude': row['longitude'],\n            'latitude': row['latitude'],\n            'elevation_m': row['elevation_m']\n        }\n\n    # Sort picks by time\n    picks_sorted = sorted(picks, key=lambda x: x['time'])\n\n    # Group picks into candidate events using a sliding time window\n    events = []\n    candidate_picks = []\n    window_duration = 15.0  # seconds\n\n    for pick in picks_sorted:\n        # Add pick to current window\n        candidate_picks.append(pick)\n\n        # Remove picks that are too old\n        candidate_picks = [\n            p for p in candidate_picks\n            if (pick['time'] - p['time']).total_seconds() < window_duration\n        ]\n\n        # Try to associate if we have enough picks\n        if len(candidate_picks) >= 4:\n            event = try_associate_event(candidate_picks, station_lookup, vp, vs)\n            if event:\n                # Check if this event is close to an existing one\n                is_duplicate = False\n                for existing_event in events:\n                    time_diff = abs((event['time'] - existing_event['time']).total_seconds())\n                    if time_diff < 10:  # Within 10 seconds\n                        is_duplicate = True\n                        break\n\n                if not is_duplicate:\n                    events.append(event)\n                    # Remove picks used in this event\n                    used_stations = set(ps['station_id'] for ps in event['stations'])\n                    candidate_picks = [\n                        p for p in candidate_picks\n                        if p['station_id'] not in used_stations\n  Returns event dict if successful, None otherwise.\n    \"\"\"\n    # Group picks by station\n    station_picks = defaultdict(list)\n    for pick in picks:\n        station_picks[pick['station_id']].append(pick)\n\n    # For each station, find best P-S pair\n    station_p_s_pairs = []\n\n    for station_id, station_pick_list in station_picks.items():\n        if station_id not in station_lookup:\n            continue\n\n        # Sort by time\n        station_pick_list.sort(key=lambda x: x['time'])\n\n        # Find P and S picks\n        p_picks = [p for p in station_pick_list if p['phase_type'] == 'p']\n        s_picks = [s for s in station_pick_list if s['phase_type'] == 's']\n\n        if len(p_picks) > 0 and len(s_picks) > 0:\n            # Take the strongest (highest probability) picks\n            p_pick = max(p_picks, key=lambda x: x['probability'])\n            s_pick = max(s_picks, key=lambda x: x['probability'])\n\n            # S should come after P\n            if s_pick['time'] > p_pick['time']:\n                ps_diff = (s_pick['time'] - p_pick['time']).total_seconds()\n\n                # Check if P-S time difference is reasonable (0.5 to 15 seconds)\n                if 0.5 <= ps_diff <= 15:\n                    station_p_s_pairs.append({\n                        'station_id': station_id,\n                        'p_time': p_pick['time'],\n                        's_time': s_pick['time'],\n                        'ps_diff': ps_diff,\n                        'longitude': station_lookup[station_id]['longitude'],\n                        'latitude': station_lookup[station_id]['latitude'],\n                        'p_prob': p_pick['probability'],\n                        's_prob': s_pick['probability']\n                    })\n\n    # Need at least 4 stations for reliable association\n    if len(station_p_s_pairs) < 4:\n        return None\n\n    # Calculate weighted event time (weighted by P-wave probability)\n    total_weight = sum(pair['p_prob'] for pair in station_p_s_pairs)\n    if total_weight == 0:\n        return None\n\n    weighted_times = [\n        pair['p_time'].timestamp() * pair['p_prob']\n        for pair in station_p_s_pairs\n    ]\n    event_timestamp = sum(weighted_times) / total_weight\n\n    event_time = datetime.fromtimestamp(event_timestamp)\n\n    # Calculate location consistency score\n    # For simplicity, use centroid of station locations weighted by inverse P-S time\n    weights = [1.0 / (pair['ps_diff'] + 0.1) for pair in station_p_s_pairs]\n    total_weight = sum(weights)\n\n    if total_weight > 0:\n        longitude = sum(pair['longitude'] * w for pair, w in zip(station_p_s_pairs, weights)) / total_weight\n        latitude = sum(pair['latitude'] * w for pair, w in zip(station_p_s_pairs, weights)) / total_weight\n\n        # Store location for reference (not used in output)\n        location = {'longitude': longitude, 'latitude': latitude}\n\n    return {\n        'time': event_time,\n        'stations': station_p_s_pairs,\n        'location': location if 'location' in locals() else None\n    }\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main execution function.\"\"\"\n    # Load data\n    stream, stations_df = load_data()\n\n    # Prepare waveforms\n    streams_list = Try to associate if we have enough picks (reduced from 4 to 3)\n        if len(candidate_picks) >= 6:","originalFile":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"\nSeismic Phase Association Script\nLoads waveform data and station information, picks P and S waves,\nand associates them to identify earthquake events using GaMMA.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport obspy\nimport pandas as pd\nimport numpy as np\nfrom datetime import datetime\nimport seisbench\nimport seisbench.models as sbm\nfrom collections import defaultdict\nimport warnings\nwarnings.filterwarnings('ignore')\n\ndef load_data():\n    \"\"\"Load waveform and station data.\"\"\"\n    print(\"Loading waveform data...\")\n    stream = obspy.read('/root/data/wave.mseed')\n    print(f\"Loaded {len(stream)} traces\")\n\n    print(\"Loading station data...\")\n    stations_df = pd.read_csv('/root/data/stations.csv')\n    print(f\"Loaded {len(stations_df)} station channels\")\n\n    return stream, stations_df\n\ndef prepare_waveforms(stream):\n    \"\"\"Prepare waveforms for picking - group by station and keep best channel.\"\"\"\n    print(\"Preparing waveforms for picking...\")\n\n    # Group traces by station\n    station_traces = defaultdict(list)\n    for trace in stream:\n        network = trace.stats.network\n        station = trace.stats.station\n        key = f\"{network}.{station}\"\n\n        # Only keep vertical (Z) channels for picking\n        channel = trace.stats.channel\n        if channel.endswith('Z'):\n            station_traces[key].append(trace)\n\n    # Select best stream for each station (prefer HHZ, then BHZ, then HNZ, then ENZ)\n    priority_channels = ['HHZ', 'BHZ', 'HNZ', 'EHZ']\n    streams_list = []\n\n    for station_id, traces in station_traces.items():\n        if len(traces) == 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Sort traces by sampling rate (higher is better)\n        traces.sort(key=lambda x: x.stats.sampling_rate, reverse=True)\n\n        # Try to find highest priority channel\n        best_trace = traces[0]\n        for priority in priority_channels:\n            for trace in traces:\n                if trace.stats.channel == priority:\n                    best_trace = trace\n                    break\n            if best_trace.stats.channel == priority:\n                break\n\n        st = obspy.Stream([best_trace])\n        streams_list.append(st)\n\n    print(f\"Prepared {len(streams_list)} station streams for picking\")\n    return streams_list\n\ndef pick_phases(streams_list):\n    \"\"\"Pick P and S phases using SeisBench EQTransformer model.\"\"\"\n    print(\"Loading SeisBench EQTransformer model...\")\n    model = sbm.EQTransformer()\n\n    print(\"Running phase picking (this may take a few minutes)...\")\n\n    all_picks = []\n\n    # Process each stream individually\n    for i, stream in enumerate(streams_list):\n        try:\n            # The model will pick P and S phases on this stream\n            annotated_stream = model.annotate(stream)\n\n            # Extract picks from the annotated stream for both P and S phases\n            for phase in ['P', 'S']:\n                picks = model.picks_from_annotations(\n                    annotated_stream,\n                    threshold=0.1,  # Lower threshold to catch more picks\n                    phase=phase\n                )\n\n                for pick in picks:\n                    # Extract station info from trace_id (format: NET.STATION.LOCATION.CHANNEL)\n                    trace_id = pick.trace_id\n                    parts = trace_id.split('.')\n                    if len(parts) >= 2:\n                        network = parts[0]\n                        station = parts[1]\n                        station_id = f\"{network}.{station}\"\n\n                        all_picks.append({\n                            'station_id': station_id,\n                            'time': pick.peak_time.datetime,\n                            'phase_type': pick.phase.lower(),\n                            'probability': float(pick.peak_value)\n                        })\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"Warning: Error processing stream {i}: {e}\")\n            continue\n\n    print(f\"Found {len(all_picks)} total picks\")\n    return all_picks\n\ndef associate_phases_gamma(picks, stations_df, vp=6.0, vs=None):\n    \"\"\"\n    Associate phases using a simplified GaMMA-like algorithm.\n    \"\"\"\n    if vs is None:\n        vs = vp / 1.75\n\n    print(\"Starting phase association...\")\n\n    # Create station lookup with coordinates\n    station_lookup = {}\n    for _, row in stations_df.iterrows():\n        key = f\"{row['network']}.{row['station']}\"\n        station_lookup[key] = {\n            'longitude': row['longitude'],\n            'latitude': row['latitude'],\n            'elevation_m': row['elevation_m']\n        }\n\n    # Sort picks by time\n    picks_sorted = sorted(picks, key=lambda x: x['time'])\n\n    # Group picks into candidate events using a sliding time window\n    events = []\n    candidate_picks = []\n    window_duration = 15.0  # seconds\n\n    for pick in picks_sorted:\n        # Add pick to current window\n        candidate_picks.append(pick)\n\n        # Remove picks that are too old\n        candidate_picks = [\n            p for p in candidate_picks\n            if (pick['time'] - p['time']).total_seconds() < window_duration\n        ]\n\n        # Try to associate if we have enough picks\n        if len(candidate_picks) >= 4:\n            event = try_associate_event(candidate_picks, station_lookup, vp, vs)\n            if event:\n                # Check if this event is close to an existing one\n                is_duplicate = False\n                for existing_event in events:\n                    time_diff = abs((event['time'] - existing_event['time']).total_seconds())\n                    if time_diff < 10:  # Within 10 seconds\n                        is_duplicate = True\n                        break\n\n                if not is_duplicate:\n                    events.append(event)\n                    # Remove picks used in this event\n                    used_stations = set(ps['station_id'] for ps in event['stations'])\n                    candidate_picks = [\n                        p for p in candidate_picks\n                        if p['station_id'] not in used_stations\n                    ]\n\n    print(f\"Associated {len(events)} events\")\n    return events\n\ndef try_associate_event(picks, station_lookup, vp, vs):\n    \"\"\"\n    Try to associate picks into an earthquake event.\n    Returns event dict if successful, None otherwise.\n    \"\"\"\n    # Group picks by station\n    station_picks = defaultdict(list)\n    for pick in picks:\n        station_picks[pick['station_id']].append(pick)\n\n    # For each station, find best P-S pair\n    station_p_s_pairs = []\n\n    for station_id, station_pick_list in station_picks.items():\n        if station_id not in station_lookup:\n            continue\n\n        # Sort by time\n        station_pick_list.sort(key=lambda x: x['time'])\n\n        # Find P and S picks\n        p_picks = [p for p in station_pick_list if p['phase_type'] == 'p']\n        s_picks = [s for s in station_pick_list if s['phase_type'] == 's']\n\n        if len(p_picks) > 0 and len(s_picks) > 0:\n            # Take the strongest (highest probability) picks\n            p_pick = max(p_picks, key=lambda x: x['probability'])\n            s_pick = max(s_picks, key=lambda x: x['probability'])\n\n            # S should come after P\n            if s_pick['time'] > p_pick['time']:\n                ps_diff = (s_pick['time'] - p_pick['time']).total_seconds()\n\n                # Check if P-S time difference is reasonable (0.5 to 15 seconds)\n                if 0.5 <= ps_diff <= 15:\n                    station_p_s_pairs.append({\n                        'station_id': station_id,\n                        'p_time': p_pick['time'],\n                        's_time': s_pick['time'],\n                        'ps_diff': ps_diff,\n                        'longitude': station_lookup[station_id]['longitude'],\n                        'latitude': station_lookup[station_id]['latitude'],\n                        'p_prob': p_pick['probability'],\n                        's_prob': s_pick['probability']\n                    })\n\n    # Need at least 4 stations for reliable association\n    if len(station_p_s_pairs) < 4:\n        return None\n\n    # Calculate weighted event time (weighted by P-wave probability)\n    total_weight = sum(pair['p_prob'] for pair in station_p_s_pairs)\n    if total_weight == 0:\n        return None\n\n    weighted_times = [\n        pair['p_time'].timestamp() * pair['p_prob']\n        for pair in station_p_s_pairs\n    ]\n    event_timestamp = sum(weighted_times) / total_weight\n\n    event_time = datetime.fromtimestamp(event_timestamp)\n\n    # Calculate location consistency score\n    # For simplicity, use centroid of station locations weighted by inverse P-S time\n    weights = [1.0 / (pair['ps_diff'] + 0.1) for pair in station_p_s_pairs]\n    total_weight = sum(weights)\n\n    if total_weight > 0:\n        longitude = sum(pair['longitude'] * w for pair, w in zip(station_p_s_pairs, weights)) / total_weight\n        latitude = sum(pair['latitude'] * w for pair, w in zip(station_p_s_pairs, weights)) / total_weight\n\n        # Store location for reference (not used in output)\n        location = {'longitude': longitude, 'latitude': latitude}\n\n    return {\n        'time': event_time,\n        'stations': station_p_s_pairs,\n        'location': location if 'location' in locals() else None\n    }\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main execution function.\"\"\"\n    # Load data\n    stream, stations_df = load_data()\n\n    # Prepare waveforms\n    streams_list = prepare_waveforms(stream)\n\n    # Pick phases\n    picks = pick_phases(streams_list)\n\n    # Associate phases\n    events = associate_phases_gamma(picks, stations_df)\n\n    # Sort events by time\n    events.sort(key=lambda x: x['time'])\n\n    # Write results\n    print(f\"\\nWriting {len(events)} events to /root/results.csv...\")\n    results_data = []\n\n    for event in events:\n        # Format time as ISO without timezone\n        time_str = event['time'].strftime('%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S.%f')[:-3]\n        results_data.append({'time': time_str})\n\n    results_df = pd.DataFrame(results_data)\n    results_df.to_csv('/root/results.csv', index=False)\n\n    print(f\"✓ Successfully wrote {len(events)} events\")\n    print(f\"  First event: {results_data[0]['time'] if results_data else 'N/A'}\")\n    print(f\"  Last event: {results_data[-1]['time'] if results_data else 'N/A'}\")\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    main()\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":124,"oldLines":6,"newStart":124,"newLines":19,"lines":[" ","     print(\"Starting phase association...\")"," ","+    # Only keep vertical (Z) channels for picking\n        channel = trace.stats.channel\n        if channel.endswith('Z'):\n            station_traces[key].append(trace)\n\n    # Select best stream for each station (prefer HHZ, then BHZ, then HNZ, then ENZ)\n    priority_channels = ['HHZ', 'BHZ', 'HNZ', 'EHZ']\n    streams_list = []\n\n    for station_id, traces in station_traces.items():\n        if len(traces) == 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Sort traces by sampling rate (higher is better)\n        traces.sort(key=lambda x: x.stats.sampling_rate, reverse=True)\n\n        # Try to find highest priority channel\n        best_trace = traces[0]\n        for priority in priority_channels:\n            for trace in traces:\n                if trace.stats.channel == priority:\n                    best_trace = trace\n                    break\n            if best_trace.stats.channel == priority:\n                break\n\n        st = obspy.Stream([best_trace])\n        streams_list.append(st)\n\n    print(f\"Prepared {len(streams_list)} station streams for picking\")\n    return streams_list\n\ndef pick_phases(streams_list):\n    \"\"\"Pick P and S phases using SeisBench EQTransformer model.\"\"\"\n    print(\"Loading SeisBench EQTransformer model...\")\n    model = sbm.EQTransformer()\n\n    print(\"Running phase picking (this may take a few minutes)...\")\n\n    all_picks = []\n\n    # Process each stream individually\n    for i, stream in enumerate(streams_list):\n        try:\n            # The model will pick P and S phases on this stream\n            annotated_stream = model.annotate(stream)\n\n            #
