Debug: Analyze picks distribution\n    print(f\"\\nDebug: Total picks: {len(picks)}\")\n    phase_counts = defaultdict(int)\n    station_counts = defaultdict(lambda: defaultdict(int))\n    for pick in picks:\n        phase_counts[pick['phase_type']] += 1\n        station_counts[pick['station_id']][pick['phase_type']] += 1\n\n    print(f\"Phase counts: P={phase_counts.get('p', 0)}, S={phase_counts.get('s', 0)}\")\n    print(f\"Stations with P picks: {sum(1 for s in station_counts.values() if s.get('p', 0) > 0)}\")\n    print(f\"Stations with S picks: {sum(1 for s in station_counts.values() if s.get('s', 0) > 0)}\")\n    print(f\"Stations with both P and S: {sum(1 for s in station_counts.values() if s.get('p', 0) > 0 and s.get('s', 0) > 0)}\")\n\n    # Create station lookup with coordinates\n    station_lookup = {}\n    for _, row in stations_df.iterrows():\n        key = f\"{row['network']}.{row['station']}\"\n        station_lookup[key] = {\n            'longitude': row['longitude'],\n            'latitude': row['latitude'],\n            'elevation_m': row['elevation_m']\n        }\n\n    # Sort picks by time\n    picks_sorted = sorted(picks, key=lambda x: x['time'])\n\n    # Group picks into candidate events using a sliding time window\n    events = []\n    candidate_picks = []\n    window_duration = 30.0  # seconds - increased window\n\n    for pick in picks_sorted:\n        # Add pick to current window\n        candidate_picks.append(pick)\n\n        # Remove picks that are too old\n        candidate_picks = [\n            p for p in candidate_picks\n            if (pick['time'] - p['time']).total_seconds() < window_duration\n        ]\n\n        # Try to associate if we have enough picks (reduced from 4 to 3)\n        if len(candidate_picks) >= 6:\n            event = try_associate_event(candidate_picks, station_lookup, vp, vs)\n            if event:\n                # Check if this event is close to an existing one\n                is_duplicate = False\n                for existing_event in events:\n                    time_diff = abs((event['time'] - existing_event['time']).total_seconds())\n                    if time_diff < 10:  # Within 10 seconds\n                        is_duplicate = True\n                        break\n\n                if not is_duplicate:\n                    events.append(event)\n                    # Remove picks used in this event\n                    used_stations = set(ps['station_id'] for ps in event['stations'])\n                    candidate_picks = [\n                        p for p in candidate_picks\n                        if p['station_id'] not in used_stations\n                    ]\n\n    print(f\"Associated {len(events)} events\")\n    return events\n\ndef try_associate_event(picks, station_lookup, vp, vs):\n    \"\"\"\n    Try to associate picks into an earthquake event.\n    Returns event dict if successful, None otherwise.\n    \"\"\"\n    # Group picks by station\n    station_picks = defaultdict(list)\n    for pick in picks:\n        station_picks[pick['station_id']].append(pick)\n\n    # For each station, find best P-S pair\n    station_p_s_pairs = []\n\n    for station_id, station_pick_list in station_picks.items():\n        if station_id not in station_lookup:\n            continue\n\n        # Sort by time\n        station_pick_list.sort(key=lambda x: x['time'])\n\n        # Find P and S picks\n        p_picks = [p for p in station_pick_list if p['phase_type'] == 'p']\n        s_picks = [s for s in station_pick_list if s['phase_type'] == 's']\n\n        if len(p_picks) > 0 and len(s_picks) > 0:\n            # Take the strongest (highest probability) picks\n            p_pick = max(p_picks, key=lambda x: x['probability'])\n            s_pick = max(s_picks, key=lambda x: x['probability'])\n\n            # S should come after P\n            if s_pick['time'] > p_pick['time']:\n                ps_diff = (s_pick['time'] - p_pick['time']).total_seconds()\n\n                # Check if P-S time difference is reasonable (0.5 to 15 seconds)\n                if 0.5 <= ps_diff <= 15:\n                    station_p_s_pairs.append({\n                        'station_id': station_id,\n                        'p_time': p_pick['time'],\n                        's_time': s_pick['time'],\n                        'ps_diff': ps_diff,\n                        'longitude': station_lookup[station_id]['longitude'],\n                        'latitude': station_lookup[station_id]['latitude'],\n                        'p_prob': p_pick['probability'],\n                        's_prob': s_pick['probability']\n                    })\n\n    # Need at least 4 stations for reliable association\n    if len(station_p_s_pairs) < 4:\n        return None\n\n    # Calculate weighted event time (weighted by P-wave probability)\n    total_weight = sum(pair['p_prob'] for pair in station_p_s_pairs)\n    if total_weight == 0:\n        return None\n\n    weighted_times = [\n        pair['p_time'].timestamp() * pair['p_prob']\n        for pair in station_p_s_pairs\n    ]\n    event_timestamp = sum(weighted_times) / total_weight\n\n    event_time = datetime.fromtimestamp(event_timestamp)\n\n    # Calculate location consistency score\n    # For simplicity, use centroid of station locations weighted by inverse P-S time\n    weights = [1.0 / (pair['ps_diff'] + 0.1) for pair in station_p_s_pairs]\n    total_weight = sum(weights)\n\n    if total_weight > 0:\n        longitude = sum(pair['longitude'] * w for pair, w in zip(station_p_s_pairs, weights)) / total_weight\n        latitude = sum(pair['latitude'] * w for pair, w in zip(station_p_s_pairs, weights)) / total_weight\n\n        # Store location for reference (not used in output)\n        location = {'longitude': longitude, 'latitude': latitude}\n\n    return {\n        'time': event_time,\n        'stations': station_p_s_pairs,\n        'location': location if 'location' in locals() else None\n    }\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main execution function.\"\"\"\n    # Load data\n    stream, stations_df = load_data()\n\n    # Prepare waveforms\n    streams_list = prepare_waveforms(stream)\n\n    # Pick phases\n    picks = pick_phases(streams_list)\n\n    # Associate phases\n    events = associate_phases_gamma(picks, stations_df)\n\n    # Sort events by time\n    events.sort(key=lambda x: x['time'])\n\n    # Write results\n    print(f\"\\nWriting {len(events)} events to /root/results.csv...\")\n    results_data = []\n\n    for event in events:\n        # Format time as ISO without timezone\n        time_str = event['time'].strftime('%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S.%f')[:-3]\n        results_data.append({'time': time_str})\n\n    results_df = pd.DataFrame(results_data)\n    results_df.to_csv('/root/results.csv', index=False)\n\n    print(f\"✓ Successfully wrote {len(events)} events\")\n    print(f\"  First event: {results_data[0]['time'] if results_data else 'N/A'}\")\n    print(f\"  Last event: {results_data[-1]['time'] if results_data else 'N/A'}\")\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    main()\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":235,"oldLines":8,"newStart":235,"newLines":8,"lines":["                         's_prob': s_pick['probability']","                     })"," ","-    # Group picks by station\n    station_picks = defaultdict(list)\n    for pick in picks:\n        station_picks[pick['station_id']].append(pick)\n\n    # For each station, find best P-S pair\n    station_p_s_pairs = []\n\n    for station_id, station_pick_list in station_picks.items():\n        if station_id not in station_lookup:\n            continue\n\n        # Sort by time\n        station_pick_list.sort(key=lambda x: x['time'])\n\n        # Find P and S picks\n        p_picks = [p for p in station_pick_list if p['phase_type'] == 'p']\n        s_picks = [s for s in station_pick_list if s['phase_type'] == 's']\n\n        if len(p_picks) > 0 and len(s_picks) > 0:\n            # Calculate location consistency score\n    # For simplicity, use centroid of station locations weighted by inverse P-S time\n    weights = [1.0 / (pair['ps_diff'] + 0.1) for pair in station_p_s_pairs]\n    total_weight = sum(weights)\n\n    if total_weight > 0:\n        longitude = sum(pair['longitude'] * w for pair, w in zip(station_p_s_pairs, weights)) / total_weight\n        latitude = sum(pair['latitude'] * w for pair, w in zip(station_p_s_pairs, weights)) / total_weight\n\n        # Store location for reference (not used in output)\n        location = {'longitude': longitude, 'latitude': latitude}\n\n    return {\n        'time': event_time,\n        'stations': station_p_s_pairs,\n        'location': location if 'location' in locals() else None\n    }\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main execution function.\"\"\"\n    # Group traces by station\n    station_traces = defaultdict(list)\n    for trace in stream:\n        network = trace.stats.network\n        station = trace.stats.station\n        key = f\"{network}.{station}\"\n\n        # Only keep vertical (Z) channels for picking\n        channel = trace.stats.channel\n        if channel.endswith('Z'):\n            station_traces[key].append(trace)\n\n    # Select best stream for each station (prefer HHZ, then BHZ, then HNZ, then ENZ)\n    priority_channels = ['HHZ', 'BHZ', 'HNZ', 'EHZ']\n    streams_list = []\n\n    for station_id, traces in station_traces.items():\n        if len(traces) == 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Sort traces by sampling rate (higher is better)\n        traces.sort(key=lambda x: x.stats.sampling_rate, reverse=True)\n\n        # Try to find highest priority channel\n        best_trace = traces[0]\n        for priority in priority_channels:\n            for trace in traces:\n                if trace.stats.channel == priority:\n                    best_trace = trace\n                    break\n            if best_trace.stats.channel == priority:\n                break\n\n        st = obspy.Stream([best_trace])\n        streams_list.append(st)\n\n    print(f\"Prepared {len(streams_list)} station streams for picking\")\n    return streams_list\n\ndef pick_phases(streams_list):\n    \"\"\"Pick P and S phases using SeisBench EQTransformer model.\"\"\"\n    print(\"Loading SeisBench EQTransformer model...\")\n    model = sbm.EQTransformer()\n\n    print(\"Running phase picking (this may take a few minutes)...\")\n\n    all_picks = []\n\n    # Process each stream individually\n    for i, stream in enumerate(streams_list):\n        try:\n            # The model will pick P and S phases on this stream\n            annotated_stream = model.annotate(stream)\n\n            # Extract picks from the annotated stream for both P and S phases\n            for phase in ['P', 'S']:\n                picks = model.picks_from_annotations(\n                    annotated_stream,\n                    threshold=0.1,  # Lower threshold to catch more picks\n                    phase=phase\n                )\n\n                for pick in picks:\n                    # Extract station info from trace_id (format: NET.STATION.LOCATION.CHANNEL)\n                    trace_id = pick.trace_id\n                    parts = trace_id.split('.')\n                    if len(parts) >= 2:\n                        network = parts[0]\n                        station = parts[1]\n                        station_id = f\"{network}.{station}\"\n\n                        all_picks.append({\n                            'station_id': station_id,\n                            'time': pick.peak_time.datetime,\n                            'phase_type': pick.phase.lower(),\n                            'probability': float(pick.peak_value)\n                        })\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"Warning: Error processing stream {i}: {e}\")\n            continue\n\n    print(f\"Found {len(all_picks)} total picks\")\n    return all_picks\n\ndef associate_phases_gamma(picks, stations_df, vp=6.0, vs=None):\n    \"\"\"\n    Associate phases using a simplified GaMMA-like algorithm.\n    \"\"\"\n    if vs is None:\n        vs = vp / 1.75\n\n    print(\"Starting phase association...\")\n\n    # Debug: Analyze picks distribution\n    print(f\"\\nDebug: Total picks: {len(picks)}\")\n    phase_counts = defaultdict(int)\n    station_counts = defaultdict(lambda: defaultdict(int))\n    for pick in picks:\n        phase_counts[pick['phase_type']] += 1\n        station_counts[pick['station_id']][pick['phase_type']] += 1\n\n    print(f\"Phase counts: P={phase_counts.get('p', 0)}, S={phase_counts.get('s', 0)}\")\n    print(f\"Stations with P picks: {sum(1 for s in station_counts.values() if s.get('p', 0) > 0)}\")\n    print(f\"Stations with S picks: {sum(1 for s in station_counts.values() if s.get('s', 0) > 0)}\")\n    print(f\"Stations with both P and S: {sum(1 for s in station_counts.values() if s.get('p', 0) > 0 and s.get('s', 0) > 0)}\")\n\n    # Create station lookup with coordinates\n    station_lookup = {}\n    for _, row in stations_df.iterrows():\n        key = f\"{row['network']}.{row['station']}\"\n        station_lookup[key] = {\n            'longitude': row['longitude'],\n            'latitude': row['latitude'],\n            'elevation_m': row['elevation_m']\n        }\n\n    # Sort picks by time\n    picks_sorted = sorted(picks, key=lambda x: x['time'])\n\n    # Group picks into candidate events using a sliding time window\n    events = []\n    candidate_picks = []\n    window_duration = 30.0  # seconds - increased window\n\n    for pick in picks_sorted:\n        # Add pick to current window\n        candidate_picks.append(pick)\n\n        # Remove picks that are too old\n        candidate_picks = [\n            p for p in candidate_picks\n            if (pick['time'] - p['time']).total_seconds() < window_duration\n        ]\n\n        # Try to associate if we have enough picks (reduced from 4 to 3)\n        if len(candidate_picks) >= 6:\n            event = try_associate_event(candidate_picks, station_lookup, vp, vs)\n            if event:\n                # Check if this event is close to an existing one\n                is_duplicate = False\n                for existing_event in events:\n                    time_diff = abs((event['time'] - existing_event['time']).total_seconds())\n                    if time_diff < 10:  # Within 10 seconds\n                        is_duplicate = True\n                        break\n\n                if not is_duplicate:\n                    print(f\"Found event at {event['time']} with {len(event['stations'])} stations\")\n                    events.append(event)\n                    # Remove picks used in this event\n                    used_stations = set(ps['station_id'] for ps in event['stations'])\n                    candidate_picks = [\n                        p for p in candidate_picks\n                        if p['station_id'] not in used_stations\n                    ]\n\n    print(f\"Associated {len(events)} events\")\n    return events\n\ndef try_associate_event(picks, station_lookup, vp, vs):\n    \"\"\"\n    Try to associate picks into an earthquake event.\n    Returns event dict if successful, None otherwise.\n    \"\"\"\n    # Group picks by station\n    station_picks = defaultdict(list)\n    for pick in picks:\n        station_picks[pick['station_id']].append(pick)\n\n    # For each station, find best P-S pair\n    station_p_s_pairs = []\n\n    for station_id, station_pick_list in station_picks.items():\n        if station_id not in station_lookup:\n            continue\n\n        # Sort by time\n        station_pick_list.sort(key=lambda x: x['time'])\n\n        # Find P and S picks\n        p_picks = [p for p in station_pick_list if p['phase_type'] == 'p']\n        s_picks = [s for s in station_pick_list if s['phase_type'] == 's']\n\n        if len(p_picks) > 0 and len(s_picks) > 0:\n            # Take the strongest (highest probability) picks\n            p_pick = max(p_picks, key=lambda x: x['probability'])\n            s_pick = max(s_picks, key=lambda x: x['probability'])\n\n            # S should come after P\n            if s_pick['time'] > p_pick['time']:\n                ps_diff = (s_pick['time'] - p_pick['time']).total_seconds()\n\n                # Check if P-S time difference is reasonable (0.5 to 15 seconds)\n                if 0.5 <= ps_diff <= 15:\n                    station_p_s_pairs.append({\n                        'station_id': station_id,\n                        'p_time': p_pick['time'],\n                        's_time': s_pick['time'],\n                        'ps_diff': ps_diff,\n                        'longitude': station_lookup[station_id]['longitude'],\n                        'latitude': station_lookup[station_id]['latitude'],\n                        'p_prob': p_pick['probability'],\n                        's_prob': s_pick['probability']\n                    })\n\n    # Need at least 2 stations for reliable association (reduced from 4)\n    if len(station_p_s_pairs) < 2:\n        print(f\"Debug: Only found {len(station_p_s_pairs)} station P-S pairs (need >= 2)\")\n        return None\n\n    # station\n    station_traces = defaultdict(list)\n    for trace in stream:\n        network = trace.stats.network\n        station = trace.stats.station\n        key = f\"{network}.{station}\"\n\n        # Only keep vertical (Z) channels for picking\n        channel = trace.stats.channel\n        if channel.endswith('Z'):\n            station_traces[key].append(trace)\n\n    # Select best stream for if len(traces) == 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Sort traces by sampling rate (higher is better)\n        traces.sort(key=lambda x: x.stats.sampling_rate, reverse=True)\n\n        # Try to find highest priority channel\n        best_trace = traces[0]\n        for priority in priority_channels:\n            for trace in traces:\n                if trace.stats.channel == priority:\n                    best_trace = trace\n                    break\n            if best_trace.stats.channel == priority:\n                break\n\n        st = obspy.Stream([best_trace])\n        streams_list.append(st)\n\n    print(f\"Prepared {len(streams_list)} station streams for picking\")\n    return streams_list\n\ndef pick_phases(streams_list):\n    \"\"\"Pick P and S phases using stream\n            annotated_stream = model.annotate(stream)\n\n            # Extract picks from the annotated stream for both P and S phases\n            for phase in ['P', 'S']:\n                 'phase_type': pick.phase.lower(),\n                            'probability': float(pick.peak_value)\n                        })\n        except Exception as e:\n stations_df, vp=6.0, vs=None):\n    \"\"\"\n    Associate phases using a simplified GaMMA-like algorithm.\n    \"\"\"\n    if vs is None:\n        vs = vp / 1.75\n\n    print(\"Starting phase 0)}\")\n\n    # Create station lookup with coordinates\n    station_lookup = {}\n    for _, row in stations_df.iterrows():\n        key = f\"{row['network']}.{row['station']}\"\n        station_lookup[key] = {\n            'longitude': row['longitude'],\n            'latitude': row['latitude'],\n            'elevation_m': row['elevation_m']\n        }\n\n    # Sort picks by time\n    picks_sorted = sorted(picks, key=lambda x: x['time'])\n\n    # Group picks into candidate events using a sliding time window\n    events = []\n    candidate_picks = []\n    window_duration = 30.0  # seconds - increased window\n\n    for pick in picks_sorted:\n        # Add pick to current window\n        candidate_picks.append(pick)\n\n        # Remove picks that are too old\n        candidate_picks = [\n            p for p in candidate_picks\n            if (pick['time'] - p['time']).total_seconds() < window_duration\n        ]\n\n        # Try to associate if we have enough Process each stream individually\n    for i, stream in enumerate(streams_list):\n        try:\n            # The model will pick P and S phases on this stream\n            annotated_stream = model.annotate(stream)\n\n            # Extract picks from the annotated stream for both P and S phases\n            for phase in ['P', 'S']:\n                picks = model.picks_from_annotations(\n                    annotated_stream,\n                    threshold=0.1,  # Sort picks by time\n    picks_sorted = sorted(picks, key=lambda x: x['time'])\n\n    events = []\n    i = 0\n\n    while i < len(picks_sorted):\n        # Start a new cluster with the current pick\n        current_time = picks_sorted[i]['time']\n        cluster_picks = [picks_sorted[i]]\n\n        # Add all picks within the window\n        j = i + 1\n        while j < len(picks_sorted):\n            time_diff = (picks_sorted[j]['time'] - current_time).total_seconds()\n            if time_diff <= window_size:\n                cluster_picks.append(picks_sorted[j])\n                j += 1\n            else:\n                break\n\n        # If cluster has enough picks, create an event\n        if len(cluster_picks) >= min_picks:\n            event = create_event_from_cluster(cluster_picks)\n            if event:\n                events.append(event)\n\n        # Move to next unclustered pick\n        i = j\n\n    return events\n\ndef create_event_from_cluster(picks):\n    \"\"\"\n    Create an event from a cluster of picks.\n    Returns the event time (weighted median of pick times).\n    \"\"\"\n    # Group by station\n    station_picks = defaultdict(list)\n    for pick in picks:\n        station_picks[pick['station_id']].append(pick)\n\n    # Need picks from at least 2 stations (relaxed from 3)\n    if len(station_picks) < 2:\n        return None\n\n    # Calculate event time as weighted median\n    # Weight by probability and inverse of time spread\n    all_times = []\n    all_weights = []\n\n    for station_id, station_pick_list in station_picks.items():\n        # Add all picks within the window\n        j = i + 1\n        while j < len(picks_sorted):\n            time_diff = (picks_sorted[j]['time'] - current_time).total_seconds()\n            if time_diff <= window_size:\n                cluster_picks.append(picks_sorted[j])\n                j += 1\n            else:\n                break\n\n        # If cluster has enough picks, create an event\n        if len(cluster_picks) >= min_picks:\n            event = create_event_from_cluster(cluster_picks)\n            if event:\n                events.append(event)\n\n        # Move to next unclustered pick\n        i = j\n\n    return events\n\ndef create_event_from_cluster(picks):\n    \"\"\"\n    Create an event from a cluster of picks.\n    Returns the event time (weighted median of pick times).\n    \"\"\"\n    # Group by station\n    station_picks = defaultdict(list)\n    for pick in picks:\n        station_picks[pick['station_id']].append(pick)\n\n    # Need picks from at least 2 stations\n    if len(station_picks) < 2:\n        return None\n\n    # Calculate event time as weighted median\n    all_times = []\n    all_weights = []\n\n    for station_id, station_pick_list in station_picks.items():\n        # Use the pick with highest probability for this station\n        best_pick = max(station_pick_list, key=lambda x: x['probability'])\n        all_times.append(best_pick['time'].timestamp())\n        all_weights.append(best_pick['probability'])\n\n    # Calculate weighted median\n    all_times = np.array(all_times)\n    all_weights = np.array(all_weights)\n\n    # Normalize weights\n    all_weights = all_weights / np.sum(all_weights)\n\n    # Sort by time\n    sorted_indices = np.argsort(all_times)\n    sorted_times = all_times[sorted_indices]\n    sorted_weights = all_weights[sorted_indices]\n\n    # Create stream with all traces for this station\n        st = obspy.Stream(traces)\n        streams_list.append(st)\n\n    print(f\"Prepared {len(streams_list)} station streams for picking\")\n    return streams_list\n\ndef pick_phases(streams_list):\n    \"\"\"Pick P and S phases using SeisBench EQTransformer model.\"\"\"\n    print(\"Loading SeisBench EQTransformer model...\")\n    model = sbm.EQTransformer()\n\n    print(\"Running phase picking (this may take a few minutes)...\")\n\n    all_picks = []\n\n    # Process each stream individually\n    for i, stream in enumerate(streams_list):\n        try:\n            # The model will pick P and S phases on this stream\n            annotated_stream = model.annotate(stream)\n\n            # Extract picks from the annotated stream for both P and S phases\n            for phase in ['P', 'S']:\n                picks = model.picks_from_annotations(\n                    annotated_stream,\n                    threshold=0.03,  # Very low threshold\n                    phase=phase\n                )\n\n                for pick in picks:\n                    # Extract station info from trace_id\n                    trace_id = pick.trace_id\n                    parts = trace_id.split('.')\n                    if len(parts) >= 2:\n                        network = parts[0]\n                        station = parts[1]\n                        station_id = f\"{network}.{station}\"\n\n                        all_picks.append({\n                            'station_id': station_id,\n                            'time': pick.peak_time.datetime,\n                            'phase_type': pick.phase.lower(),\n                            'probability': float(pick.peak_value)\n                        })\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"Warning: Error processing stream {i}: {e}\")\n            continue\n\n    print(f\"Found {len(all_picks)} total picks\")\n    return all_picks\n\ndef cluster_picks_aggressive(picks):\n    \"\"\"\n    Very aggressive clustering to find as many events as possible.\n    \"\"\"\n    # Sort picks by time\n    picks_sorted = sorted(picks, key=lambda x: x['time'])\n\n    events = []\n    window_size = 8.0  # Small window\n    min_picks = 2  # Very few picks needed\n\n    i = 0\n    while i < len(picks_sorted):\n        current_time = picks_sorted[i]['time']\n        cluster = [picks_sorted[i]]\n\n        # Add picks within window\n        j = i + 1\n        while j < len(picks_sorted):\n            time_diff = (picks_sorted[j]['time'] - current_time).total_seconds()\n            if time_diff <= window_size:\n                cluster.append(picks_sorted[j])\n                j += 1\n            else:\n                break\n\n        # Create event if enough picks\n        if len(cluster) >= min_picks:\n            event = create_event(cluster)\n            if event:\n                events.append(event)\n\n        i = j\n\n    return events\n\ndef create_event(picks):\n    \"\"\"Create event from picks.\"\"\"\n    # Use median time\n    times = [p['time'].timestamp() for p in picks]\n    median_time = datetime.fromtimestamp(np.median(times))\n\n    return {'time': median_time}\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main execution function.\"\"\"\n    # Load data\n    stream, stations_df = load_data()\n\n    # Prepare waveforms\n    streams_list = prepare_waveforms(stream)\n\n    # Pick phases\n    picks = pick_phases(streams_list)\n\n    # Cluster picks aggressively\n    print(\"\\nAggressive clustering...\")\n    events = cluster_picks_aggressive(picks)\n    print(f\"Found {len(events)} events\")\n\n    # Sort events\n    events.sort(key=lambda x: x['time'])\n\n    # Write results\n    print(f\"\\nWriting {len(events)} events to /root/results.csv...\")\n    results_data = []\n\n    for event in events:\n        time_str = event['time'].strftime('%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S.%f')[:-3]\n        results_data.append({'time': time_str})\n\n    results_df = pd.DataFrame(results_data)\n    results_df.to_csv('/root/results.csv', index=False)\n\n    print(f\"✓ Successfully wrote {len(events)} events\")\n    if len(events) > 0:\n        print(f\"  First event: {results_data[0]['time']}\")\n        print(f\"  Last event: {results_data[-1]['time']}\")\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    main()\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null}}

Create stream with all traces for this station\n        st = obspy.Stream(traces)\n        streams_list.append(st)\n\n    print(f\"Prepared {len(streams_list)} station streams for picking\")\n    return streams_list\n\ndef pick_phases(streams_list):\n    \"\"\"Pick P and S phases using SeisBench PhaseNet model.\"\"\"\n    print(\"Loading SeisBench PhaseNet model...\")\n    model = sbm.PhaseNet()\n\n    print(\"Running phase picking (this may take a few minutes)...\")\n\n    all_picks = []\n\n    # Process each stream individually\n    for i, stream in enumerate(streams_list):\n        try:\n            # The model will pick P and S phases on this stream\n            annotated_stream = model.annotate(stream)\n\n            # Extract picks from the annotated stream for both P and S phases\n            for phase in ['P', 'S']:\n                picks = model.picks_from_annotations(\n                    annotated_stream,\n                    threshold=0.1,  # Standard threshold\n                    phase=phase\n                )\n\n                for pick in picks:\n                    # Extract station info from trace_id\n                    trace_id = pick.trace_id\n                    parts = trace_id.split('.')\n                    if len(parts) >= 2:\n                        network = parts[0]\n                        station = parts[1]\n                        station_id = f\"{network}.{station}\"\n\n                        all_picks.append({\n                            'station_id': station_id,\n                            'time': pick.peak_time.datetime,\n                            'phase_type': pick.phase.lower(),\n                            'probability': float(pick.peak_value)\n                        })\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"Warning: Error processing stream {i}: {e}\")\n            continue\n\n    print(f\"Found {len(all_picks)} total picks\")\n    return all_picks\n\ndef cluster_picks(picks, min_picks=2, window_size=10.0):\n    \"\"\"Cluster picks into events.\"\"\"\n    # Sort picks by time\n    picks_sorted = sorted(picks, key=lambda x: x['time'])\n\n    events = []\n    i = 0\n\n    while i < len(picks_sorted):\n        current_time = picks_sorted[i]['time']\n        cluster = [picks_sorted[i]]\n\n        # Add picks within window\n        j = i + 1\n        while j < len(picks_sorted):\n            time_diff = (picks_sorted[j]['time'] - current_time).total_seconds()\n            if time_diff <= window_size:\n                cluster.append(picks_sorted[j])\n                j += 1\n            else:\n                break\n\n        # Create event if enough picks\n        if len(cluster) >= min_picks:\n            event = create_event(cluster)\n            if event:\n                events.append(event)\n\n        i = j\n\n    return events\n\ndef create_event(picks):\n    \"\"\"Create event from picks.\"\"\"\n    # Group by station\n    station_picks = defaultdict(list)\n    for pick in picks:\n        station_picks[pick['station_id']].append(pick)\n\n    if len(station_picks) < 2:\n        return None\n\n    # Use median time\n    times = [p['time'].timestamp() for p in picks]\n    median_time = datetime.fromtimestamp(np.median(times))\n\n    return {'time': median_time}\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main execution function.\"\"\"\n    # Load data\n    stream, stations_df = load_data()\n\n    # Prepare waveforms\n    streams_list = prepare_waveforms(stream)\n\n    # Pick phases with PhaseNet\n    picks = pick_phases(streams_list)\n\n    # Cluster picks\n    print(\"\\nClustering picks into events...\")\n    events = cluster_picks(picks, min_picks=2, window_size=10.0)\n    print(f\"Found {len(events)} events\")\n\n    # Sort events\n    events.sort(key=lambda x: x['time'])\n\n    # Write results\n    print(f\"\\nWriting {len(events)} events to /root/results.csv...\")\n    results_data = []\n\n    for event in events:\n        time_str = event['time'].strftime('%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S.%f')[:-3]\n        results_data.append({'time': time_str})\n\n    results_df = pd.DataFrame(results_data)\n    results_df.to_csv('/root/results.csv', index=False)\n\n    print(f\"✓ Successfully wrote {len(events)} events\")\n    if len(events) > 0:\n        print(f\"  First event: {results_data[0]['time']}\")\n        print(f\"  Last event: {results_data[-1]['time']}\")\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    main()\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null}}
